Preserved sanitized research - no implementation

Markdown export Level of Detail

WI-052 | 261113 -5 | Local, Agent Host and Copilot CLI evidence

RecommendationControl catalogueRepository formatsOnline researchObserved chats and exportsDecisionsFiles
Preservation edition: source locators and dynamic/private field names are anonymized. Detailed inventories and counts are kept, but anonymized commands cannot identify the original chats. Use the synthetic examples for portable review. Real export bundles are available only in the ignored private archive on the originating machine. See preservation details and sample guide.

Recommendation and boundaries

Keep today's detailed default. Start with independent tool-input and tool-result controls, readable reasoning, question/answer handling and per-tool overrides. Extend only after reviewing real exports.

Default stays detailed

No named whole-export profiles. Choose content and detail independently.

MCP is a tool origin

All tool calls already includes MCP. MCP-only is a filter, not an extra duplicated block.

Short does not mean complete

Excerpts, exclusions, source gaps and upstream clipping need distinct labels.

Keep research local

Exports and companions can contain private text and images. No report upload is required.

Coverage statement: this report lists the types found in the cited public sources and the bounded local survey. It does not claim all possible historical chat formats, tools, sessions or renderer support. Online API types, internal persistence types and actual sampled data are separate evidence.

Design rules

  • MCP calls are tool calls. Do not render a call twice because both selectors match.
  • Separate content selection, detail level, collapse state and companion-file inclusion.
  • None, short, medium and complete describe content detail, not whole-export profiles.
  • Complete means all supported recorded content for that category, not raw-source completeness.
  • Use recorded invocation descriptions or deterministic arguments. Do not add a summarizing model call.
  • Keep call identity, order, status, errors, cancellation and exclusion counts intelligible at reduced detail.
  • Classify by recorded provenance and payload first. Tool-name matching is a fallback and must be labelled.
  • Do not infer missing history, fetch current instruction files, dereference remote attachments or decrypt opaque reasoning.
  • Redaction and privacy are independent. Hiding an inline block while retaining its companion does not remove its data.
  • Unknown new types still need drift reporting. An exclusion rule must not silence an unsupported format.

Read this report

Filter table rows below. Clear the filter to restore everything. Expand sections for full evidence. Source and census JSON files remain linked for independent review. Counts in raw mutation/event inventories are not counts of unique rendered calls.

Input, result and attachment detail need separate controlsSDK documentation describes result.content, detailedContent and contents, while MCP supports text/image/audio/resource/resource_link plus structuredContent.A tool's concise model-facing text is not necessarily its full display result. A no-results choice must also govern newly generated result companions.https://github.com/github/copilot-sdk/blob/ed814e4134fdba86e2e88b82dcac85b1c125ed8a/docs/features/streaming-events.md
Ephemeral is an availability boundary, not a way to restore past outputThe SDK reference marks live-only event families, including user-input/elicitation and shell-output channels.An offline export can select only what was recorded. Do not promise complete historical questions, live deltas or per-call usage merely because the live API defines them.https://github.com/github/copilot-sdk/blob/ed814e4134fdba86e2e88b82dcac85b1c125ed8a/docs/features/streaming-events.md
Question/answer output deserves a semantic categoryLocal questionCarousel state, agent-native user_input events, MCP elicitation and tool-based ask_user use different records. The Local stress example has an unmatched recorded answer string despite a general verification pass.Short form should keep title plus recorded outcome. Show skipped/cancelled/unavailable separately. A string nonmatch is a warning, not conclusive proof of why content is missing.corpus/findings.json
Memory is a documented feature, but not one export formatGitHub documents repository memories and user preferences. Historical content must come from recorded evidence, not today's memory state.https://docs.github.com/en/copilot/concepts/prompting-and-context/copilot-memory

Recommended first implementation proposal, not approval

  • Keep the current detailed default byte-stable where existing deterministic rules apply.
  • Offer independent tool-input and tool-result detail, with exact per-tool overrides.
  • Treat MCP as an origin selector inside tools, not a second output category.
  • Add recorded readable reasoning on/off and a dedicated question/answer representation.
  • Keep errors/status, source warnings and intentional-exclusion accounting intelligible.
  • Defer shell-based file-read inference, a full rule language, extra media decoding and reopened hook/permission policy.

Proposed controls (41)

Names and values describe ideas, not approved CLI flags. Short/medium/complete are per-category or per-tool levels, not whole-export presets. Start with fewer controls rather than exposing every stored field.

CoreDetailed defaultExisting behaviorKeep unchangedExisting exports and automation keep their meaning.Do not relabel today's export as lossless or claim pending fidelity flags exist.
CorePrompts and repliesKeep; optional future role filterKeep as core content in the first phaseA reader can follow the conversation.System-initiated requests and hidden turns need separate classification. Do not remove instructions embedded in ordinary user text by pattern.
ToolsTool selectionAll; include exact tools; exclude exact toolsSupport exact stable identity, with unknown names reportedUser chooses specific noisy tools.Aliases and renamed tools can make a rule stop matching. Report unmatched selectors.
ToolsTool input detailNone; short; medium; completeIndependent of result detailCalls can remain understandable without pages of arguments.Input can include code, full delegated prompts, paths or credentials. Redact before extracting excerpts.
ToolsTool result detailNone; short; medium; completeIndependent of input detailCovers all calls with no results and compact-result requests.An empty result is not the same as a deliberately excluded result or missing source.
ToolsPer-tool overrideExact tool identity -> input/detail/result/attachment settingsOne canonical rule per toolFile reads can be short while failures and edits stay complete.Do not introduce a full rule language before simpler selectors prove insufficient.
ToolsMCP origin filterAll; MCP only; non-MCP only; per recorded server/toolMCP as subset, not second output streamCovers MCP-only calls with or without responses.A name prefix does not prove MCP transport. Origin can be missing or serialized differently.
ToolsTool call lineRecorded description plus identifying arguments and statusDeterministic short representationMatches requested one-line summaries.Display descriptions can omit actual arguments. Label excerpted commands and never call them the full executed command.
ToolsErrors and cancellationKeep status; short/full error evidenceKeep visible even when normal results are disabledA failed call cannot look successful.Needs approval as a result-filter exception. Error messages may contain sensitive text.
ToolsRepeated lifecycle updatesCurrent merge rules; future delta summaryKeep current identity/order rulesLess duplicate status/output.Never deduplicate by matching text alone or combine different call IDs.
ToolsProgress and asynchronous outputNone; milestones; complete recorded updatesResearch separately from final resultsReduces repeated command polling.A late failure or new output can occur after an initial tool response.
FilesFile readsNone; one-line path/range; preview; completeTreat as a semantic role with exact-tool overridesDirectly supports one-line reads and no reads.Shell/MCP tools may also read files. Static command parsing cannot reliably classify arbitrary programs.
FilesSearch resultsQuery only; query/count/paths; excerpts; completeSeparate queries from result bodiesSearch often dominates long exports.Missing matches and zero matches must not be confused. Preserve whether a result was truncated upstream.
FilesEdits and patchesPaths/status; summary; complete recorded patchKeep distinct from file readsShows changes without all patch text when desired.A path list cannot reconstruct edits. Never generate old file contents from today's working tree.
FilesEdit review actionsSummary; complete available recordsInventory accept/reject/undo separatelyDistinguishes proposed edits from accepted edits.Recorded edit intent does not prove final disk contents or developer acceptance.
ReasoningReadable reasoningNone; complete; optional excerptFirst phase: on/off, no invented summarySupports all/no reasoning.A recorded model-written summary and a full thinking block are not equivalent. Keep source labels.
ReasoningOpaque reasoningCount/reason onlyNever export opaque continuation tokens as readable thoughtExplains unavailable reasoning.Cannot decrypt or reconstruct. Not a richer selectable detail level.
QuestionsUser questions and answersTitle + answer; question/options + answer; complete structured exchangeDedicated category, not just another tool-result blobThe short form can show the decision clearly.Answers can be in tool results, question-carousel state, or later user turns. Preserve free text, skipped/cancelled and unanswered separately.
QuestionsQuestion UI dataHide layout; keep options/selected labels/validation as availableDo not require UI decoration for readable answersAvoids noisy schemas.MCP elicitation form data and a custom ask_user tool have different schemas.
AgentsSub-agent conversationsDelegation line; recorded result summary; complete verified nested conversationSeparate delegation input from child transcript detailLarge child traces can be shortened selectively.A returned summary is not a full transcript. Parent-child mapping must stay verified.
AgentsDelegation promptShort description; excerpt; completeDefault remains complete under current policyHelps explain a child's assignment.Changing the current always-complete child-prompt rule needs approval.
AgentsAgent metadataName/role; model/effort/timing; supported extra fieldsKeep identity labels on any retained child outputReader knows which agent acted.Do not assign parent usage, effort or model to a child without recorded attribution.
ContextInstructionsRecorded references; excerpt; complete recorded contentSeparate system/developer/project/user instructions where source distinguishes themAllows less instruction boilerplate.Loaded instructions may be absent from persisted records. Do not read current files to claim historical content.
ContextSkills and custom agentsInvocation/name only; recorded description; complete loaded contentSeparate discovery, invocation and injected textShows workflow choice without repeating long instructions.Reading SKILL.md does not prove a runtime skill invocation. Custom-agent definitions can contain private text.
ContextMemoryOperation/title; recorded content; complete supported payloadSeparate read/write/delete/vote from loaded contextExplains memory influence and state-changing operations.Graph lookup, saved Copilot memory, AGENTS.md and compaction are not one memory format. Never query current memory to reconstruct the past.
ContextAttachments and referencesLabels/URI/range; excerpt; complete recorded textSeparate attached content from assistant citationsDocuments input context.Paths can be private. A link does not prove the bytes were recorded or are available now.
ContextCompaction and context transferMarker; summary; complete recorded compactionKeep boundary visibleExplains a discontinuity in later replies.A compaction summary is not the missing original conversation.
MediaImages and screenshotsMetadata/reference; linked bytes; future inline displayKeep current companion behavior as defaultControl large visual evidence independently.Image pixels bypass text redaction. Binary inclusion and inline presentation are separate choices.
MediaAudio/video/non-image dataMetadata; recorded transcript; supported companionsInventory now; defer extraction beyond current policyMakes documented upstream types visible as future work.Documented MCP audio support does not prove local persistence or exporter support. Do not decode arbitrary payloads by default.
MediaMCP resources and structured outputResource identity; selected fields; complete recorded structured contentKeep content blocks, structuredContent and errors distinctAvoids flattening structured responses into unreadable text.Resource links differ from embedded resources. Do not fetch a URI while exporting offline.
PresentationCode blocks and diagramsKeep; references/excerpts in non-default modeKeep complete by defaultCode and Mermaid often carry the main answer.Naive line clipping breaks fences, indentation, tables and diagrams.
PresentationCitations and referencesNone; labels/targets; complete recorded citation metadataKeep source links with retained statementsMaintains evidence behind an answer.Removing citations can leave claims unsupported. Link safety/trust metadata is not prose.
PresentationStructured UI outputPlain summary; supported semantic rendering; recorded JSON fallbackInventory trees, commands, plans, reviews and notebook edits separatelyCaptures content beyond Markdown prose.UI widgets can carry decisions. Generic JSON fallback is not the same as a semantic renderer.
PresentationCollapse stateExpanded; collapsed by categorySeparate from LODLess scrolling without losing content.Collapsed Markdown is still present, searchable and potentially private.
ArtifactsCompanion-file policyCurrent preview + full companion; future no companion for excluded contentMake exclusion apply to newly generated companion files tooA smaller export bundle is actually smaller.Leaving a hidden full companion defeats content exclusion. Do not delete earlier exports or caches.
MetadataModels, modes and effortNone; per-turn labels; complete supported recordsSeparate selected model from recorded answering modelExplains routing and model changes.Auto/Fusion records vary by version. Unrecorded effort must remain unknown.
MetadataUsage and creditsNone; session summary; turn/agent detail; supported raw metadataKeep source and coverage labels with any numbersReduce cost tables independently.Never add cumulative checkpoints, mix Local units with nano-AIU, or treat missing as zero.
MetadataTimingNone; timestamps/duration; detailed intervalsKeep recorded timing distinct from export timeLess turn-header noise.Waiting, execution and wall-clock durations differ. Stable rendering needs an explicit context.
DiagnosticsWarnings, source gaps and exclusionsShort summary plus detailed manifestDo not offer a silent mode that removes accountabilityReaders know what lower LOD removed.Counts need units: events, calls, text blocks, images and bytes are not interchangeable.
DiagnosticsHooks, permissions and model snapshotsKeep existing exclusions; optional future researchNo inclusion change in the first phaseAvoids reopening unrelated fidelity policy.Can duplicate conversation or expose secrets. Documented events are not automatically export content.
ConfigurationSettings file and rule priorityDefault < global category < exact per-tool override; final exclusion wins within one resolved ruleProposed ordering only; decide before implementationMany per-tool choices are easier to reuse than a long command.Ambiguous include/exclude precedence can silently hide data. Reject contradictory rules rather than guessing.

Repository format background

Documented in this repository, not a fresh runtime census. See online and corpus inventories for newly checked evidence. Source: docs/CHAT-SESSION-SCHEMA.md:453-999.

The repository schema guide contains older statements that conflict with current README/SPEC behavior. In particular, its SDK-not-implemented and old clipping statements are not treated as current runtime evidence. See local export results for observed behavior.

Online documentation and public source

Public APIs describe what an extension or server can emit. Serialization source describes what may survive on disk. Neither establishes that every sampled session contains those records. Exact checked sources and version caveats follow.

S21fetched 2026-11-13 (public preview feature)2026-11-13Official feature docs: GitHub Copilot Memory stores two types -- repository-level facts (coding conventions, architectural decisions, build commands) and user-level personal preferences. Facts are cited back to supporting code; preferences may be cited with direct quotes of what the user said. Unused entries auto-expire after 28 days unless revalidated. T43Session event envelope (ephemeral vs persisted)The single most direct LOD control found in any source this session: every copilot-sdk event carries ephemeral:true (or absent=false). Ephemeral events (deltas, progress, session.idle, elicitation/user_input/permission... see per-event notes) stream live but are never written to the session log and are NOT replayed on resume; persisted events are durable and replayable. parentId chains only persisted events together.S22sdk-protocol{id:string(uuid), timestamp:string(iso8601), parentId:string|null, agentId?:string, ephemeral?:boolean, type:string, data:object}Public API (official copilot-sdk docs page). Per-event ephemeral/persisted status given in the per-event notes below is as documented on this page; not cross-checked against generated SDK source.

Attachment[] item shape not itself detailed on this page (name-only: file/directory/selection/blob/GitHub-reference).
T45assistant.message (+ turn_start/turn_end/message_delta) and reasoning-vs-opaque fieldsassistant.message carries content plus three parallel, provider-specific opaque/plain reasoning fields on the SAME message: reasoningOpaque (Anthropic encrypted thinking), encryptedContent (OpenAI encrypted reasoning), reasoningText (readable reasoning text), plus phase('thinking'|'response'). This is the CLI/SDK-side analogue to VS Code's includeEncryptedThinking flag (T9) -- confirms the opaque-vs-readable reasoning split is a cross-harness, cross-provider concept, not VS Code-specific.S22reasoningdata:{messageId, content, toolRequests?:ToolRequest[], reasoningOpaque?, reasoningText?, encryptedContent?, phase?, outputTokens?, interactionId?, parentToolCallId?(deprecated, use envelope agentId)}Public API. toolRequests[] item: {toolCallId, name, arguments?, type?:'function'|'custom'}.
T46assistant.reasoning / assistant.reasoning_deltaSeparate persisted-complete-block vs ephemeral-delta pair for extended thinking, matching the same complete/delta pattern used for assistant.message/assistant.message_delta -- a general streaming-LOD pattern applied consistently across content kinds.S22reasoningassistant.reasoning data:{reasoningId, content}; assistant.reasoning_delta (ephemeral) data:{reasoningId, deltaContent}Public API.
T47tool.execution_start/progress/shell_output/execution_complete and 3-tier result contenttool.execution_complete.result has three explicit LOD tiers at once: content (concise, may be truncated for token efficiency, sent to the LLM), detailedContent (full, preserves complete diffs etc., for display), and contents (ContentBlock[] structured text/terminal/image/audio/resource). tool.shell_output is a 4th, ephemeral, append-only live-terminal channel explicitly NOT persisted/replayed, superseding the deprecated tool.execution_partial_result bounded-snapshot channel.S22tools-mcptool.execution_start data:{toolCallId, toolName, arguments?, mcpServerName?, mcpServerDisplayName?, mcpToolName?}; tool.execution_complete data:{toolCallId, success, result?:{content, detailedContent?, contents?:ContentBlock[]}, error?:{message,code?}, toolTelemetry?, isUserRequested?}Public API. MCP identity resolved as mcpConfigServerName ?? mcpServerName (legacy-compatible) per doc note; ContentBlock[] item shape not itself detailed on this page.
T48subagent.started/completed/failed/selected/deselectedA fuller subagent lifecycle schema than VS Code's name-only ChatSubagentToolInvocationData (T-gap): started carries agentName/agentDisplayName/agentDescription/model; completed/failed add durationMs/totalTokens/totalToolCalls; selected is an inferred-routing event (no toolCallId) with a tools:string[]|null scope (null = all tools); deselected has an explicitly empty {} payload.S22subagentssubagent.started:{toolCallId,agentName,agentDisplayName,agentDescription,model?}; subagent.completed:{toolCallId,agentName,agentDisplayName,model?,durationMs?,totalTokens?,totalToolCalls?}; subagent.failed: same + error; subagent.selected:{agentName,agentDisplayName,tools:string[]|null}; subagent.deselected:{}Public API. Envelope-level agentId (T43) is the correlation mechanism for routing sub-agent assistant/tool events to a separate UI lane; parentToolCallId on assistant/tool events is explicitly marked deprecated in favor of agentId.
T49skill.invokedConfirms Skills (SKILL.md, T36/T38 gap) are delivered to the model as fully-injected content, not a reference/pointer: content holds the full skill text. allowedTools documents the skill's requested auto-approval scope but the doc is explicit that the SDK does not auto-approve them -- the host's permission handler still decides (CLI auto-approves while active; other hosts may not).S22skillsdata:{name, path, content, allowedTools?:string[], pluginName?, pluginVersion?}Public API. Resolves part of the earlier SKILL.md gap (event-level shape now known); the SKILL.md frontmatter schema itself is still not directly verified.
T50permission.requested/completed (incl. kind:"memory")CORRECTS prior gap framing: GitHub Copilot Memory IS a publicly documented feature (S21), and the SDK's own schema for the write-side of a memory action is this permission kind: {subject, fact, citations}, matching the docs' statement that facts/preferences are cited back to supporting code or quotes. No separate dedicated memory.* event family (e.g. memory.created/memory.retired) was found in this pass -- the approval-flow event is the only memory-adjacent schema documented here. Other kinds (shell/write/read/mcp/url/custom-tool) are the general tool/file/network approval LOD gate; result.kind has 5 named outcomes incl. distinct denied-by-rules vs denied-interactively-by-user vs denied-by-content-exclusion-policy.S22

T51user_input.requested/completed and elicitation.requested/completedTwo parallel ephemeral ask/answer event pairs: user_input.* for free-text/choice questions the agent itself originates, elicitation.* specifically for MCP's elicitation/create protocol (mode:'form') plus a non-MCP mode:'url' (open a browser URL) -- i.e. the CLI/SDK host renders both agent-native questions and MCP elicitation requests through a related-but-separate event-family, unlike VS Code's single ChatQuestion/ChatResponseQuestionCarouselPart (T-existing) which does not distinguish an MCP origin.S22question-answeruser_input.requested:{requestId,question,choices?,allowFreeform?,toolCallId?}; user_input.completed:{requestId,answer?,wasFreeform?}; elicitation.requested:{requestId,message,mode?:'form'|'url',requestedSchema?,url?,elicitationSource?,elicitationSourceDisplayName?,toolCallId?}; elicitation.completed:{requestId,action?:'accept'|'decline'|'cancel',content?}Public API, both marked Ephemeral. in the source doc.
T52Session lifecycle: idle/compaction/usage_info vs usage_checkpoint/shutdownsession.usage_info (ephemeral, per-snapshot context-window utilization: tokenLimit/currentTokens/systemTokens/conversationTokens/toolDefinitionsTokens) is explicitly a DIFFERENT, lower-durability tier than session.usage_checkpoint (NOT marked ephemeral; described as a 'Durable aggregate usage checkpoint used to reconstruct accounting when a session is resumed' -- totalNanoAiu, totalPremiumRequests). session.compaction_start/complete brackets context-compaction with a rich result (preCompactionTokens/postCompactionTokens/messagesRemoved/summaryContent/checkpointNumber/checkpointPath). session.shutdown aggregates whole-session metrics (modelMetrics per model, codeChanges linesAdded/linesRemoved/filesModified, totalApiDurationMs).S22progresssession.idle(ephemeral):{aborted?,mode?}; session.compaction_start(ephemeral):{}; session.compaction_complete:{success,error?,preCompactionTokens?,postCompactionTokens?,preCompactionMessagesLength?,messagesRemoved?,tokensRemoved?,summaryContent?,checkpointNumber?,checkpointPath?,compactionTokensUsed?,requestId?}; session.usage_info(ephemeral):{tokenLimit,currentTokens,messagesLength,systemTokens?,conversationTokens?,toolDefinitionsTokens?,isInitial?}; session.usage_checkpoint:{totalNanoAiu,totalPremiumRequests?}; session.shutdown:{shutdownType:'routine'|'error',errorReason?,totalPremiumRequests?,totalApiDurationMs,sessionStartTime,codeChanges,modelMetrics,currentModel?}Public API. Directly answers the task's progress + compaction + usage-persisted-vs-ephemeral questions at the CLI/SDK layer.
T53assistant.usage (per-API-call usage/cost, ephemeral)Rich, fully field-level public usage schema distinct from, and more granular than, the session-aggregate T52 events: per-call inputTokens/outputTokens/reasoningTokens (subset of outputTokens)/cacheReadTokens/cacheWriteTokens, cost (billing multiplier), duration, timeToFirstTokenMs/interTokenLatencyMs (streaming perf), reasoningEffort, apiEndpoint (incl. distinguishing /chat/completions vs /v1/messages vs /responses vs ws:/responses vs /interactions), quotaSnapshots and copilotUsage (itemized provider cost breakdown), contentFilterTriggered/finishReason.S22models-usagedata:{model,inputTokens?,outputTokens?,reasoningTokens?,cacheReadTokens?,cacheWriteTokens?,cacheExpiresAt?,contentFilterTriggered?,finishReason?,cost?,duration?,timeToFirstTokenMs?,interTokenLatencyMs?,reasoningEffort?,initiator?,apiCallId?,serviceRequestId?,apiEndpoint?,providerCallId?,quotaSnapshots?,copilotUsage?}Public API. Marked Ephemeral.; resolves prior T40 gap (per-call usage fields) at the CLI/SDK layer -- VS Code's own ChatResult usage shape remains separately unverified.
T54SerializedChatResponsePart (VS Code internal persisted-history union)The actual on-disk/history-serialization part union is narrower than the live ChatResponseStream part union (vscodeTypes.ts, T-existing): only 9 named members (markdown, file-tree, inline-reference, markdown-with-vulnerability, thinking, a generic serialized-progress-content wrapper, question-carousel, plan-review, disabled-Claude-hooks). This is a concrete, line-cited content-vs-persisted-history LOD narrowing point -- many live-stream part types (confirmation, warning, command-button, etc.) are evidently NOT part of this persisted union by name, implying they are either excluded from history or folded into the generic IChatProgressResponseContentSerialized wrapper (wrapper internals not opened).S23turns-historytype SerializedChatResponsePart = IMarkdownString | IChatResponseProgressFileTreeData | IChatContentInlineReference | IChatAgentMarkdownContentWithVulnerability | IChatThinkingPart | IChatProgressResponseContentSerialized | IChatQuestionCarousel | IChatPlanReview | IChatDisabledClaudeHooksPartINFERRED INTERNAL SOURCE -- workbench-internal (chatModel.ts), not an exported vscode.d.ts public API. Member interfaces themselves (e.g. IChatProgressResponseContentSerialized) not individually opened.
T55IToolInvocation / IToolInvocationContext (VS Code internal tool-call request shape)Internal request-side tool-call record carries subAgentInvocationId (subagent UI attribution, explicitly called a stop-gap pending unification with thinking/tool-call-group rendering), preToolUseResult (hook integration point, ties directly to the ChatHookType.PreToolUse hook from the earlier pass), preApproved:ConfirmedReason (lets a host/server-side decision skip the WaitingForConfirmation UI state entirely -- a direct confirmation-LOD bypass), and traceparent/tracestate W3C trace-context fields explicitly forwarded to MCP tool calls as _meta.traceparent per MCP SEP-414 (a concrete, named MCP-spec cross-reference not previously found).S24tools-mcpIToolInvocation:{callId,toolId,toolSessionResource?,originatingChatResource?,parameters,tokenBudget?,context,chatRequestId?,chatInteractionId?,chatStreamToolCallId?,subAgentInvocationId?,toolSpecificData?,modelId?,userSelectedTools?,selectedCustomButton?,preToolUseResult?,preApproved?,traceparent?,tracestate?}; IToolInvocationContext:{sandboxNetworkRestrictions?,sessionResource,requestId?,workingDirectory?}INFERRED INTERNAL SOURCE -- workbench-internal, not public API. toolSpecificData member interfaces (IChatTerminalToolInvocationData, IChatSessionTargetData, IChatAutomationConfigurationData, etc.) confirmed to exist by name in this union only, not individually opened (consistent with existing NAME-ONLY caveats elsewhere).
T56IToolResult (VS Code internal 3-kind content union)Internal result-side content union has only 3 kinds -- promptTsx (opaque prompt-element JSON, re-stringified for accessibility), text (value:string, optional audience?/title?), data (mimeType+VSBuffer, optional audience?/title?) -- narrower than and differently shaped from the public MCP 5-type content union (text/image/audio/resource/resource_link, S1): image/audio/binary are all folded into kind:data generically by mimeType rather than given distinct kinds. Separately, toolResultDetails.mcpOutput is explicitly 'Raw MCP tool result for MCP App UI rendering' -- i.e. the raw upstream MCP CallToolResult is preserved verbatim alongside the lossier internal 3-kind summary, a documented full-fidelity escape hatch.S24tools-mcpIToolResult:{content:(IToolResultPromptTsxPart|IToolResultTextPart|IToolResultDataPart)[],toolSpecificData?,toolResultMessage?,toolResultDetails?:IToolResultInputOutputDetails|IToolResultOutputDetails,toolResultError?,toolMetadata?,confirmResults?}; IToolResultTextPart:{kind:'text',value,audience?,title?}; IToolResultDataPart:{kind:'data',value:{mimeType,data:VSBuffer},audience?,title?}; IToolResultInputOutputDetails:{input,inputLanguage?,output,isError?,mcpOutput?}INFERRED INTERNAL SOURCE -- workbench-internal, not public API. LanguageModelPartAudience (the audience? type) was previously NAME-ONLY (T-gap); its referenced use here is additional but still not a full definition read.
controls (18)
  • MCP: outputSchema/structuredContent presence is itself an LOD switch -- a tool can offer both a terse text block and a full structured object (S1).
  • MCP: progress 'total' and 'message' are optional per-notification, a fine/coarse LOD choice per update (S2).
  • MCP: elicitation requestedSchema is restricted to flat primitives -- a protocol-level LOD ceiling, not a client choice (S3).
  • VS Code hooks: ChatHookResultKind success/error/warning is a 3-level severity LOD per hook call; stopReason vs systemMessage vs silent success are distinct UI-visibility levels (S8).
  • VS Code hooks: ChatRequestHooks exposes resolved shell commands (cwd/env/timeout) on the request -- a high-sensitivity LOD axis with no documented redaction opt-out found (GAP).
  • VS Code reasoning: includeEncryptedThinking is an explicit binary LOD switch (opaque/encrypted vs plain) for carrying reasoning state across requests (S9).
  • VS Code reasoning: LanguageModelThinkingPart (batch, LM-API side) vs thinkingProgress()/ChatResponseThinkingProgressPart (streaming delta, chat-response side) appear to be two separate LOD channels for the same concept -- existence confirmed, exact relationship not (S7,S9,S10).
  • VS Code tool auto-approval: documented autoApprove/autopilot spectrum controls confirmation UI (T19) volume per tool call; full enum not captured (GAP).
  • VS Code auto-routing (fusion-adjacent): ChatAutoModeTier (efficiency/balance/intelligence/fast) + ChatResponseAutoModeResolutionPart give a documented 4-level LOD for which-model-was-picked transparency, repeatable per turn (S10).
  • VS Code history LOD: ChatResponseTurn2.response explicitly excludes metadata-only stream parts, a documented content/metadata split for persisted history (S11).
  • Hooks are explicitly NOT portable across harnesses (Local vs Copilot vs Claude vs Codex each have distinct hook schema/location) despite shared file-naming conventions -- an LOD/compatibility trap called out directly in docs (S19).
  • Agent Plugins 1.0 draws an explicit standard-vs-client-specific line: Skills and MCP servers are portable; Agents, Hooks, Automation templates, Slash commands are client-specific under com.github.copilot -- a documented LOD/portability boundary (S18).
  • copilot-sdk event envelope: the ephemeral boolean flag (T43) is the SDK's single clearest, explicit LOD control -- it is the documented switch between live-only/non-replayable detail and durable/replayable detail, applied consistently across assistant/tool/session/permission/elicitation event families (S22).
  • copilot-sdk tool results: a documented 3-tier LOD per tool call -- content (concise, token-efficient, LLM-facing, may be truncated) vs detailedContent (full, display-facing) vs contents (structured ContentBlock[]) -- gives an explicit, named precedent for a text/detailed/structured LOD split (S22, T47).
  • copilot-sdk usage: two separate durability tiers for cost/usage data -- session.usage_info (ephemeral, per-snapshot context-window view) vs session.usage_checkpoint (durable, resume-reconstructable aggregate) vs assistant.usage (ephemeral, full per-call billing/perf detail) -- a 3-way usage LOD split, more granular than anything found on the VS Code extension-API side (S22, T52, T53).
  • copilot-sdk permission.completed.result.kind enumerates 5 distinct approval/denial outcomes (approved, denied-by-rules, denied-interactively-by-user, denied-no-approval-rule-and-could-not-request-from-user, denied-by-content-exclusion-policy) -- a finer confirmation-outcome LOD than the 3-tier ChatHookResultKind found earlier (S22, T50).
  • VS Code internal: preApproved:ConfirmedReason on IToolInvocation is a documented mechanism for skipping the confirmation UI state entirely when a decision was already resolved out-of-band (e.g. agent-host server-side auto-approval) -- a concrete confirmation-LOD bypass distinct from the public autoApprove/autopilot spectrum (S24, T55).
  • VS Code internal: toolResultDetails.mcpOutput explicitly preserves the raw upstream MCP CallToolResult verbatim alongside the lossier internal 3-kind IToolResult.content summary -- a documented full-fidelity escape hatch co-existing with a lower-LOD default summary (S24, T56).

gaps (10)
  • Memory (UPDATED): GitHub Copilot Memory IS a publicly documented feature (S21: repository-level facts + user-level preferences, cited, 28-day auto-expiry) and the copilot-sdk documents an event-level schema for the write/approval side (S22, T50: permission.requested kind:"memory" = {subject,fact,citations}). Remaining gap: no dedicated persisted memory.* event family (e.g. memory.created/retired) was found distinct from the approval flow, and the underlying storage/retrieval/similarity-matching mechanism is not documented at a schema level in any source read.
  • Custom instructions page and SKILL.md frontmatter schema (agent-plugins.org) were referenced but not fetched -- exact field-level LOD controls unknown (T36,T38).
  • ChatResult's own field list was not opened; any usage/telemetry/cost fields on a chat result are unverified (T40).
  • ChatToolInvocationPart, ChatMcpToolInvocationData, McpToolInvocationContentData, LanguageModelToolMCPSource, LanguageModelToolExtensionSource, ChatResponseReferencePart/2, ChatResponseWarningPart, ChatResponseInfoPart, ChatErrorLevel, ChatResponseProgressPart2, ChatResponseVoiceProgressStage, Notebook/Workspace/ExternalEditPart class bodies confirmed to exist by name only -- NAME-ONLY/PARTIAL, not verified API shape.
  • microsoft/vscode-copilot-chat is archived (S13); its historical file tree was not re-opened to diff pre-archival public API vs current extensions/copilot-in-monorepo surface.
  • The openai/codex git component pinned in cgmanifest.json (S6) is a dependency/license-inventory record only (corrected framing) -- it does not establish which runtime executes Copilot turns or the extension's full successor history; its functional role in the running extension was not explored further and remains UNKNOWN, not merely INFERRED.
  • MCP spec pages for Sampling, Roots, Prompts, Resources (beyond fragments on the Tools page) were not individually fetched; S17 claims about MCP deprecating Sampling/Roots not cross-verified against MCP's own wording.
  • Follow-up: github/copilot-sdk docs/features/streaming-events.md (S22) was fetched directly as a primary source and is now cited extensively (T43-T53). This is a hand-maintained reference page, not the generated SessionEvent union/type source itself -- the page explicitly states it does not cover every event type and that the generated per-language union is the authoritative exhaustive list; that generated schema file was not opened in this bounded follow-up.
  • ChatResponseAutoModeResolutionPart/ChatAutoModeTier (T28) is the closest analogue found to model-fusion; no multi-model ensembling/voting/fusion mechanism was found documented anywhere checked -- may not exist publicly, or exists under different terminology not yet located.
  • VS Code internal chatModel.ts/languageModelToolsService.ts citations (S23,S24; T54-T56) are INFERRED INTERNAL SOURCE, not public extension API -- useful for understanding current persisted-history/tool-result shape, but these types are not part of vscode.d.ts and may change without any public API deprecation notice.

countMeaning: Occurrence counts are aggregate inventory occurrences within the named scope. Raw mutations/events can repeat logical content. Final Local positions and actual tool identities have separate units.

normalization: Array indices and numeric keys become []. UUID/hash/call-ID map keys become {id}; absolute path/URI map keys become {path}. No primitive text values are stored except short discriminator, tool-name, state and version labels.

actual

count: 298003

unit: UTF-8 bytes

policy: Keep every kind, event type, tool name and discriminator entry. Trim only the optional field-path/type sampler to fit. Full evidence files remain unchanged.

Open the examples

Markdown and logs are linked in the file index. Keep sibling asset folders beside each Markdown file. Use VS Code Markdown preview to inspect nested details, code blocks and companion links.

Decisions before implementation

  • When results are excluded, should errors still show a short message, or status only?
  • Should short and medium use fixed lines/bytes, category-specific fields, or user-set limits? No thresholds approved.
  • Does no file reads mean excluding returned file contents, hiding read calls, or both?
  • Which recorded fields establish MCP origin across Local and SDK sources?
  • Should excluded full content be absent from new companions, while frozen source captures remain independent diagnostics?
  • How should missing user answers and unsupported event types affect reduced-detail verification?
  • Which loaded instruction/memory/skill records actually survive in each source, and which are just references?
  • How should exact tool aliases, unmatched selectors and contradictory overrides be reported?

Phased next steps

stageworkeffortnotApproved
1 - Agree semanticsReview examples, category inventory and default behavior. Decide whether errors override result exclusion.Small research/design phaseNo CLI syntax or code changes.
2 - Small coherent implementation proposalTool input/results, MCP selection, reasoning, questions and exact per-tool overrides, with exclusion accounting and saved-file verification.Medium to large; estimate after tracing both renderersNo permission to implement yet.
3 - Optional extensionSemantic file/search roles, instructions/skills/memory, structured widgets, richer media and reusable configuration.Several separate items if selectedDo not bundle these into the first implementation.

Limits and counterarguments

  • This is a bounded survey, not an exhaustive census of every Copilot installation or historical version.
  • Repository schema documentation includes older evidence and stale statements. For example, its provider section says SDK Markdown is not implemented, but the README documents implemented SDK export. It also mentions old 24 KiB clipping. These statements are not treated as current runtime evidence.
  • The knowledge graph reports metadata changes on the read documentation. Findings rely on direct reads, not graph completeness. No production call graph is claimed.
  • Cross-session history queries returned no rows for the requested recent window. The corpus survey uses read-only on-disk evidence instead.
  • Export self-checks and Layer D do not establish complete support for every documented upstream content type.
  • Report and examples may contain private paths or chat content. Keep the run local and review before sharing.

Cheaper alternative: keep all content and change collapse state alone. This reduces scrolling, not bundle size or disclosure. The stronger alternative is deterministic per-tool content controls with clear exclusions, at the cost of more parsing and verification work.

Verification design to research

  • Resolve a content policy once, before rendering and companion creation. Keep default policy behavior unchanged.
  • Record selected categories, exact tool rules and excluded counts in the archive. Keep unavailable source content separate.
  • Verify selected text, order, call identity, errors and companions against independent source evidence. Do not weaken existing full-export checks to make reduced exports pass.
  • Test default output equality, input/result combinations, global/per-tool conflicts, unknown tools, MCP attribution, questions/answers, multi-part results, nested agents and sidecar exclusions.
  • Use shared semantic tests across Local and SDK formats. Pairwise combinations can supplement, not replace, high-risk end-to-end cases.
